Anchored regex lowers to All unnecessarily high false-positive rate ^foo$ has no interior trigram; must emit trigrams from foo substring inspect lower() output for anchored patterns
Fuzz target non-termination libFuzzer timeout PostingList::iter follows corrupt next-pointer loop add iteration count guard; assert DocId strictly increases
Criterion warm-up too short benchmark variance > 5% OS page cache cold pre-warm by running query once before timed loop
Conformance fixture drift CI fails after unrelated change expected.jsonl generated from a different corpus revision re-generate expected.jsonl only via pinned script; check sha256 in CI
Delta encoding sign error near u32 max DocId underflow on decode unsigned subtraction wraps enforce I2 strictly: assert prev < current in encoder

14.7 Performance Concerns

  • Differential test I/O: grep on 50 GB corpus is the bottleneck; parallelize over subdirectories.
  • Soundness fuzz throughput: trigram extraction + lowering + 100-doc eval per iteration; keep each iteration < 1 ms to reach 1M/s.
  • Bench corpus must fit in RAM for stable p99; 10k files × 10 KB avg = 100 MB, well within bounds.
  • ASAN overhead in shard fuzz: 2–5x slower; run with 1 GB memory limit to detect runaway allocation.
  • Criterion: disable ASLR (setarch -R) for reproducible cache behaviour in CI.
  • Index build benchmark: parallelism gated by rayon thread count; pin to 8 threads for reproducibility.
  • varint decode hot path: unroll 1-byte and 2-byte cases; branch-predictor-friendly (most deltas < 128).

14.8 Edge Cases

  • Regex that matches empty string (e.g., a*): lowering must not produce a posting list requiring zero trigrams to match; TrigramQuery::All is correct; differential test must show no false negatives.
  • Binary files in corpus: grep skips them; csearch-ingest must also skip (I5); diff test must exclude binary paths from comparison set.
  • File with zero trigrams (length < 3): must never appear in any posting list; grep also produces zero matches; diff test passes trivially.
  • Shard with n_docs=0: valid; shard fuzz must not crash on empty shard.
  • Corpus file modified between grep run and csearch ingest: use sha256 snapshot; pin corpus files read-only during test.
  • Unicode normalization: grep -rE with LC_ALL=C treats bytes; csearch indexes bytes; must be consistent.
  • Result cap interacts with differential test: set cap to MAX_INT during correctness testing to ensure no truncation hides false negatives.

14.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
Soundness violation found by fuzzer assertion panic in fuzz_soundness regression in csearch-trigquery::lower bisect regex lowering logic; fix lower(); re-run fuzz to convergence
Memory unsafety in shard reader ASAN report in fuzz_shard_fuzz potential RCE if shard bytes are attacker-controlled fix bounds check; audit all unchecked slice indexing in csearch-shard
False negative in diff test diff output has grep-only lines bug in verify, lowering, or doc-store isolate to single file + query; run csearch-cli doctor on shard
Benchmark regression > 5% criterion output fails CI gate performance degradation merged perf record; flamegraph; identify hot path change
Pack roundtrip failure property test assertion varint encoder/decoder asymmetry print failing DocId sequence; add as regression fixture

14.10 Operational Notes

  • Fuzz targets run in CI with a 5-minute budget per target, full overnight run via scheduled job; crash artifacts committed to fuzz/artifacts/ for regression tracking.
  • Differential test corpus pinned by sha256 manifest in fixtures/corpora/sha256sums.txt; CI fetches and verifies before running.
  • Benchmark results stored as JSON in bench/baseline/; CI compares against baseline on main branch.
  • csearch-cli doctor subcommand: validates all shards in manifest (CRC check, header parse, posting-list sample decode); exit 0 if all healthy.
  • Metrics from hardening runs fed into a Grafana dashboard (shard count, false-positive ratio trend, p99 latency trend).
  • Deprecation: after Phase 14 passes, mark any allow(unsafe) blocks in csearch-shard with a comment citing the fuzz coverage justification. Release gate: all four test categories (conformance, soundness fuzz, shard fuzz, bench regression) must be green before tagging a release.

Threat Model

Trust Boundaries

[Untrusted: query strings from CLI/HTTP clients]
          |
          v PARSE (csearch-query)
[Semi-trusted: parsed QueryAst / TrigramQuery]
          |
          v EXECUTE (csearch-search)
[Trusted: shard files written by indexed pipeline]
          |
          v READ (csearch-shard mmap)
[Trusted: OS page cache / filesystem]

[Untrusted: repo source files on disk] ---> csearch-ingest ---> [Trusted: shard]
[Operator: manifest, shard directory] ---> csearch-incremental ---> [Trusted: shard]

Trust boundary crossings: (1) network/CLI query string → parse boundary; (2) filesystem repo bytes → ingest boundary; (3) shard bytes → mmap decode boundary.

Threat Table

# Threat Vector Mitigations Detection Recovery
T1 Regex DoS (ReDoS) Malicious /(.+)+/ query via HTTP or CLI csearch-regex uses Thompson NFA / Pike VM only (I4); no backtracking engine reachable; enforce NFA state count cap (~8192 states) PikeVm invocation counter > expected per doc; latency spike on verify stage Drop query; return 400; log NFA size
T2 TrigramQuery amplification Regex with many alternations generates exponential OR nodes TrigramQuery size cap (node count ≤ 4096); if exceeded, degrade to All (safe: P2 soundness preserved) TrigramQuery node count metric Degrade to full scan, log degradation
T3 Malformed shard / memory-safety Attacker replaces shard bytes on disk (symlink, TOCTOU, shared volume) CRC check on open (ShardHeader.crc); mmap bounds checked on every PostingReader decode; all arithmetic is saturating; fuzz target on shard reader (Phase 14) CRC mismatch → reject shard; decode panic in debug builds Reject shard; alert operator; restore from backup manifest
T4 Path traversal in ingest Repo contains symlinks pointing outside repo root csearch-ingest resolves real path; rejects if outside root; no follow-outside-root flag by default Log skipped symlinks with reason Operator reviews skip log
T5 Binary / large-file bomb Multi-GB file ingested; trigrams fill all memory Size cap per file (configurable, default 10 MB); binary detection (null-byte sniff + MIME); mmap-backed accumulator with memory budget (P5) Ingest log: "skipped: oversized / binary" Restart ingest; adjust cap
T6 Posting-list poisoning Crafted source file maximizing trigram collisions; posting lists grow huge Per-shard posting list length cap; rare-trigram cost model in csearch-trigquery lowers query cost; query result cap (P6) Shard stat: avg/max posting list length; trigram frequency histogram Raise frequency threshold to exclude degenerate trigrams
T7 Manifest TOCTOU / partial swap Two concurrent writers corrupt manifest Manifest write uses atomic rename (I7); shard registry holds reference-counted handles; old shards unmap only when refcount=0 fsync + rename errors in log Re-read manifest; alert if CRC of new manifest invalid
T8 Query result exfiltration Server returns source text beyond match span MatchSpan returns only the matched line text, not surrounding content; API paging is bounded; no full-file dump endpoint Audit API response sizes Restrict API ACL; add auth layer outside csearch-server
T9 Excessive candidate DoS Query lowering to All (e.g., .) triggers full scan across all docs Per-query deadline (P6); result cap early-exit; candidate count limit (configurable); All query is logged with elevated severity Candidate count metric per query; deadline timeouts Rate limit All-class queries; require auth for unrestricted queries
T10 Supply-chain / dependency risk Malicious crate via Cargo dependency chain Minimize external deps (regex, memmap2, serde_json, rayon only); pin versions in Cargo.lock; audit with cargo audit; no proc-macros in hot path cargo audit in CI Pin; patch; vendor if needed
T11 SHA collision / content-address collision Two different files produce same sha256 Treat sha256 as a strong fingerprint; document birthday-attack impracticality; do not deduplicate content across repos (only per-doc identity) Log warning if collision detected at ingest Flag as anomaly; store both under distinct DocIds
T12 Shard directory write privilege escalation Process writing shards needs FS write; compromise allows arbitrary writes Run server as read-only process; ingest as separate process with write access only to shard dir; principle of least privilege Process audit; file ownership checks Separate indexer / server UIDs
T13 HTTP query injection Newlines / null bytes in query string parsed unexpectedly csearch-query sanitizes input at parse boundary; rejects null bytes; length cap 4096 bytes per query Parse error log Return 400; log raw query (truncated)
T14 Incremental index drift sha+mtime check skips a changed file sha256 is the authoritative identity (I5); mtime is only a hint to trigger sha recompute; full-sha scan available as fallback Diff between re-index and current manifest Run full rescan with csearch-cli doctor --recheck-sha

Failure Modes & Recovery

# Class Symptoms Root Causes Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery Operator Actions Forensic Artifacts Long-term Mitigation
F1 Shard CRC failure Search returns 0 results for affected shard; CRC error log Disk corruption; partial write; wrong shard file copied CRC on shard open (ShardHeader.crc) Shard removed from active registry; queries proceed with remaining shards Re-trigger ingest for affected repos csearch-cli doctor --verify-crc Shard CRC error log; fsck output Redundant storage; periodic CRC sweep via doctor
F2 OOM during ingest Ingest process killed by OOM killer Huge repo; no memory budget cap; runaway posting accumulator RSS monitoring; process exit code Ingest restarts from last manifest checkpoint Reduce batch size; enable spill-to-disk csearch-cli index --spill-threshold 512M Kernel OOM log; last manifest Tune memory budget; add swap or build-time spill
F3 Manifest corruption / partial swap Server sees zero shards; queries fail Crash mid-rename; filesystem error Server logs "empty manifest"; health check fails Server retries manifest read N times; falls back to previous symlink Restore previous manifest from backup; re-swap csearch-cli doctor --restore-manifest Last-known-good manifest (keep 3 generations) Atomic rename + fsync; manifest backup rotation
F4 Posting list decode panic Search crashes / 500 on specific queries Varint overflow; truncated shard; out-of-bounds mmap read Panic log with shard + trigram ID Affected query returns 500; other queries unaffected Remove offending shard; re-index csearch-cli doctor --decode-test <shard> Panic backtrace; shard byte range Bounds-checked decode; fuzz target (Phase 14 T3)
F5 PikeVm linear-time guarantee violated Query hangs; server goroutine / thread blocked > deadline Bug in NFA compiler (cycles not properly bounded) Per-query verify-stage latency metric; deadline enforcement Deadline fires; query returns partial results with warning File bug; identify regex pattern Disable regex class that triggers; block at parse boundary Slow-query log with regex + doc Property test + fuzz on NFA compiler; state count cap
F6 Shard mmap growth (fd/address space exhaustion) Server OOM or EMFILE; new shard load fails Unbounded shard count; no compaction fd count metric; EMFILE errors LSM compaction triggered if shard count > threshold (I3) Manual merge: csearch-cli merge --all csearch-cli stat --shards /proc/fd listing; shard directory listing Compaction threshold tuning; shard count SLO alert
F7 Trigram-query soundness violation (false negative) Query returns no match for a doc that contains the pattern Bug in csearch-trigquery lowering; incorrect OR expansion Differential test vs grep -rE (Phase 14); soundness fuzz None (correctness bug; no automatic fix) Identify offending regex; patch lowering Run differential test suite; file P0 Differential test log; offending regex + doc Soundness fuzz harness in CI; property test I1
F8 Symbol index staleness sym: queries return outdated or missing symbols Incremental rebuild missed changed files; sha drift Symbol search miss rate; diff vs re-index Full repo rescan triggered by doctor csearch-cli index --force-rescan <repo> Review incremental log for skipped files Incremental change log Periodic forced full-sha rescan; monitor drift metric
F9 High false-positive rate (many candidates, few verified) Queries slow; candidate/verified ratio >> 10 Short search string; many rare-but-coincident trigrams; highly degenerate corpus Candidate count / verified count ratio metric per query Cost-model in csearch-trigquery raises threshold Increase trigram frequency threshold csearch-cli stat --trigram-freq Per-trigram frequency histogram Cost-model tuning; block single-trigram queries above FP threshold
F10 Disk full during shard write Ingest aborted; partial shard on disk Insufficient shard directory quota Disk usage metric; write error log Ingest rolled back; partial shard unlinked Free space; re-run ingest csearch-cli doctor --cleanup-partial Partial shard files (*.tmp) Pre-flight disk space check before ingest; quota alert
F11 Concurrent manifest swap + active query Query sees stale shard list; results inconsistent Missing atomic hand-off; reference count race Query returning results from deleted shard Refcount prevents unmap until query finishes (I7) None needed if I7 upheld Audit manifest swap code path In-flight query log at swap time Invariant tests on manifest registry; integration test with concurrent load
F12 Language detection mis-classification Wrong lang: filter results; symbol extraction skipped Extension ambiguity; missing shebang; binary masquerading as text Ingest log: "lang:unknown" rate metric Fall back to lang:unknown; still index trigrams Add extension rule to lang map csearch-cli doctor --lang-audit <repo> Ingest lang-detection log Extend lang map; add content-sniff heuristics
F13 Server crash under high concurrency 500 errors; process restart Shared mutable state in shard registry under lock contention; panic in fan-out Crash log; health check failure Supervisor (systemd/k8s) restarts process; manifest re-read on startup Tune worker thread count; reduce shard fan-out Review thread pool settings Panic backtrace; thread dump Arc<RwLock> on registry; async fan-out with backpressure

Operational Readiness

Metrics Strategy

All metrics emitted via a csearch_metrics registry (Prometheus-compatible, pull endpoint at /metrics). Every query emits a span with the fields below.

Metric Type Labels Purpose
csearch_query_duration_seconds Histogram stage (parse/trigram/intersect/verify/rank), query_type Per-stage latency breakdown
csearch_query_candidates_total Counter shard_id Candidate DocIds before verify
csearch_query_verified_total Counter shard_id DocIds that passed PikeVm
csearch_query_results_total Counter query_type, result_cap_hit Delivered results; cap saturation
csearch_shard_count Gauge — Active shards in registry
csearch_shard_size_bytes Histogram shard_id On-disk shard sizes
csearch_ingest_docs_total Counter repo, lang Indexed documents
csearch_ingest_skipped_total Counter reason (binary/oversized/symlink) Skips during ingest
csearch_trigram_query_nodes Histogram — TrigramQuery node count; overflow to All rate
csearch_manifest_swap_total Counter result (ok/error) Atomic manifest swaps
csearch_crc_error_total Counter shard_id Shard CRC failures
csearch_fd_count Gauge — Open file descriptors (mmap handles)
csearch_verify_latency_p99_seconds Gauge — P99 PikeVm verify time; backtrack-safety sentinel

Tracing Strategy

  • Structured tracing via tracing crate; spans: query, intersect, verify, rank.
  • query span carries: raw query string (truncated 256B), query_id (UUID), shard_ids touched.
  • verify span carries: doc_id, shard_id, verify_ns; emitted only when > 1ms (sampling).
  • Export: OTLP to Jaeger / Tempo; sampling rate configurable (default 1% full trace, 100% on error).
  • Span attribute csearch.candidate_count and csearch.verified_count on every query span.

Structured Logging

  • Format: JSON lines; fields: ts, level, module, query_id, shard_id, msg, err.
  • Levels: ERROR for CRC failures, decode panics, manifest errors; WARN for shard count threshold, lang:unknown rate; INFO for ingest completion, manifest swap; DEBUG for per-doc trigram count (disabled in prod).
  • Slow-query log: any query with verify_stage_ms > 500 logged at WARN with full query AST.
  • Ingest log: per-repo summary (docs indexed, skipped, duration) at INFO.

Dashboard Design

Panel Visualization Alert Threshold
Query P50/P95/P99 latency Histogram heatmap P99 > 500ms → warn; > 2s → page
Candidate / Verified ratio Time series Ratio > 50 → warn
Shard count Gauge > 64 → warn (trigger compaction)
CRC error rate Counter delta Any → page
Ingest docs/hour Rate gauge Drop > 50% from baseline → warn
FD count Gauge > 80% of ulimit -n → warn
csearch_trigram_query_nodes overflow rate Rate > 1% of queries → warn

SLO Definitions

SLO Target Window Burn-rate Alert
Query success rate (non-5xx) 99.9% 30-day rolling 5× burn over 1h; 2× over 6h
Query P99 latency ≤ 500ms 99% of queries 7-day rolling Latency budget burn alert
Index freshness (lag since last commit) < 5 min Continuous Lag > 15 min → warn; > 1h → page
Shard CRC health 100% shards pass CRC At open + daily sweep Any failure → page
False-negative rate 0% (I1 invariant) CI differential test Test failure → block deploy

Alerting Philosophy

  • Page only on user-impacting events (search failure, false negatives, SLO breach).
  • Warn on leading indicators (shard count growth, candidate ratio rise, fd pressure).
  • Every alert has a runbook link in the annotation.
  • No alert without a documented owner and a response SLA.
  • Silence windows for planned ingest jobs (suppress ingest-lag alert during full rescan).

Incident Response

  1. Triage: check /metrics CRC errors, 5xx rate, shard count, verify P99.
  2. Contain: if CRC error → remove shard from manifest; if OOM → restart ingest with lower budget.
  3. Diagnose: csearch-cli doctor → shard health report; slow-query log; OTLP traces.
  4. Recover: see runbooks below.
  5. Post-mortem: five-whys; add detection to differential test or fuzz harness.

Runbooks

Runbook Trigger Key Steps
RB-01: Shard CRC failure CRC error alert csearch-cli doctor --verify-crc; remove bad shard from manifest; re-index affected repos
RB-02: OOM during ingest OOM kill Lower --spill-threshold; enable spill; restart ingest
RB-03: Manifest corruption Empty manifest / health fail Restore previous manifest generation; csearch-cli doctor --restore-manifest <gen>
RB-04: False negative detected Differential test fails Identify offending regex; bisect csearch-trigquery lowering; patch + re-deploy
RB-05: High false-positive rate Candidate/verified ratio > 50 csearch-cli stat --trigram-freq; raise frequency threshold; rebuild trigquery cost model
RB-06: Shard count exceeded Shard count > 64 alert csearch-cli merge --strategy lsm; monitor merge progress; verify new manifest CRC
RB-07: Disk full Write error during ingest csearch-cli doctor --cleanup-partial; free space; resume ingest
RB-08: Server crash loop k8s restart > 3 in 5 min Check panic backtrace; reduce worker thread count; roll back binary if regression
RB-09: Index freshness lag Lag > 15 min Check ingest process status; re-trigger csearch-cli index --repos <changed>; verify manifest swap
RB-10: Symbol index staleness sym: miss rate spike csearch-cli index --force-rescan <repo> --symbol-only; verify SymbolEntry postings

Backup and Restore Strategy

  • Shard directory: periodic snapshot (daily) to object storage (S3-compatible); shards are immutable so incremental copy (only new shards) is correct.
  • Manifest: keep last 3 generations on disk; upload each generation to object store on swap.
  • Restore: copy shards from object store; write manifest pointing to restored shards; csearch-cli doctor --verify-crc before serving.
  • No transaction log needed: shards are deterministically reproducible from source repos.

Deployment Topology

[Ingest nodes] --write--> [Shard directory (NFS / local)] <--mmap-- [Search nodes]
                                          |
                               [Manifest file (atomic rename)]
                                          |
                              [csearch-server (HTTP, fan-out)]
                                          |
                              [Load balancer / API gateway]
  • Ingest and server are separate processes; server is read-only.
  • Multiple server replicas read from shared shard directory (NFS) or replicated local dirs.
  • Manifest swap propagates to all replicas via inotify / polling (hot-reload, I7).

Canary and Rolling Upgrade

  • Canary: route 5% of traffic to new binary; compare P99 latency, 5xx rate, candidate ratio vs baseline for 30 min.
  • Rolling: deploy one server replica at a time; health check before next; rollback if P99 > 2× baseline.
  • Shard format upgrade: old binary reads old shards; new binary emits new-version shards; version field in ShardHeader; old shards remain readable until compacted away.
  • Rollback: revert binary; old shards still present; manifest unchanged; no shard migration needed.

Migration Strategy

  • On-disk format changes increment ShardHeader.version; old readers reject unknown versions with clear error.
  • Query API: additive JSON fields are non-breaking; removed fields require major API version bump.
  • Incremental migration: run new ingest in parallel; flip manifest when new shards cover all repos.

Compatibility & Versioning

Shard Format Compatibility

Aspect Policy
ShardHeader magic 8-byte constant; never changes; reader rejects mismatched magic
ShardHeader version u16; incremented on any on-disk layout change; readers reject unsupported versions with descriptive error
Trigram table layout Binary-searchable sorted array; element size fixed; version gate any extension
Posting list encoding Varint + delta (ascending DocId, I2); encoding algorithm is frozen for a given version; new compression (e.g., PFor) requires version bump
DocMeta record Fixed-offset fields up to version boundary; new fields appended at end of record with version guard; old readers skip unknown trailing bytes
CRC algorithm CRC32C (Castagnoli); never changed; stored in ShardHeader.crc
Shard immutability Once written and CRC-sealed, bytes never patched in place (I3); any change requires new shard + manifest swap

Wire / HTTP API Compatibility

Aspect Policy
JSON field additions Non-breaking; clients must ignore unknown fields
JSON field removal Breaking; requires major API version bump (/v2/...)
Query parameter additions Non-breaking with defaults
Streaming response format Newline-delimited JSON; format frozen per API major version
Error codes Numeric codes stable per major version; message strings are advisory
API versioning scheme URL-path major version (/v1/search); minor/patch changes are additive only

Type Evolution Policy

Type Evolution Rule
DocId(u32) u32 fixed; shard DocId space is per-shard; (ShardId, DocId) is the global unique key — adding more shards never exhausts DocId space
Trigram(u32) 24-bit pack is bijective (I8); format frozen; changing would invalidate all shards
QueryAst enum New variants added with a version gate; old parsers reject unknown variants with parse error
TrigramQuery enum New variants may be added; old executors treat unknown variant as All (sound degradation per P2)
MatchSpan New fields appended; existing fields never reordered or removed within a major wire version
RankScore(f32) Score magnitude may change between versions; only relative ordering is stable (P8); clients must not hardcode thresholds
ShardHeader Fields append-only past the fixed header prefix; magic + version prefix is immutable

Semantic Versioning Policy

Component SemVer Scope Breaking Change Definition
csearch-core Library crate; SemVer 2.0 Any public type signature change, removed variant, narrowed trait bound
csearch-shard on-disk format Versioned separately via ShardHeader.version Any layout change that invalidates existing shard reads
csearch-server HTTP API URL-path major version Field removal, semantic change to existing field, changed error code meaning
csearch-cli subcommand flags Minor: new flags; Major: removed/renamed flags Removed or semantically changed flags are breaking
Internal crate APIs (non-pub) No external guarantee Free to change without version bump

Rolling Upgrade Guarantees

  • Server and ingest binaries may differ by ≤1 minor version during rolling upgrades.
  • New ingest binary writes shards the old server can read (backward-compatible shard version N → N reads).
  • Forward-compatibility (old ingest, new server) is not guaranteed; always upgrade server last.
  • Manifest format is stable across minor versions; major version changes include a migration step documented in the release notes.
  • During a rolling upgrade, the shard registry hot-reload (I7) ensures no query sees a partially-swapped manifest.

Deprecation Policy

Artifact Deprecation Notice Removal
Shard format version N Announced at version N+1 release; readers emit WARN for old shards Removed at N+3; csearch-cli migrate-shards available through N+2
API endpoint /v1/ Deprecated when /v2/ ships; 12-month grace period Removed after grace; 410 Gone returned
CLI flags --deprecated-flag logs WARN with replacement; 2 minor versions Removed at next major
Public crate types #[deprecated] with replacement note; 1 minor version minimum Removed at next major

Migration Guarantees

  • Shard migration: csearch-cli migrate-shards --from-version N --to-version M rewrites all shards in-place (new shard, atomic swap, old shard retained until verified); idempotent.
  • No migration required for additive changes (new DocMeta fields, new QueryAst variants with defaults).
  • Cross-version query semantics: a query that returned result set R on version N returns a superset of R on version N+1 (I1 soundness never regresses; ranking may change).
  • Index reproducibility: given the same source files and the same binary version, the ingest pipeline produces byte-identical shards (deterministic DocId assignment per Phase 4).

Testing & Verification Strategy

Unit Tests

Module What How
csearch-core Trigram pack/unpack bijection (I8) proptest: random 3-byte sequences; assert pack→unpack roundtrip
csearch-trigram Overlapping window correctness Fixed inputs; count == len-2 trigrams; dedup preserves set
csearch-trigram Case-fold trigram inclusion Assert folded trigrams ⊇ unfolded for known inputs
csearch-shard PostingWriter→PostingReader roundtrip Random ascending DocId sequences; delta+varint encode/decode
csearch-shard CRC acceptance / rejection Flip one byte; reader must reject
csearch-regex NFA state machine on anchored patterns Unit fixtures: ^foo$, a+b, [a-z]{3}, alternation
csearch-regex Linear-time bound Time 100 × a?^n a^n patterns; assert no super-linear growth
csearch-trigquery Soundness obligation (I1) For each fixture regex R and document D: if R.is_match(D) then lower(R).eval(trigrams(D))
csearch-query Parser round-trip Parse→AST→format→reparse; AST equality
csearch-rank Score monotonicity Deeper path < shallower; test file < non-test; vendor < non-vendor
csearch-incremental SHA identity: identical content → no-op re-index Write file; index; overwrite identical bytes; manifest unchanged

Integration Tests

  • Index→search round-trip: ingest a synthetic 500-file repo, build shard, run 20 representative queries; assert exact MatchSpan sets.
  • Multi-shard fan-out: build 3 shards from disjoint repos; fan-out query; union result set == single-shard baseline.
  • Deletion via supersession (I9): add doc, index, delete doc, verify query returns nothing; compaction removes shard entry.
  • Manifest atomicity (I7): hammer concurrent searches during shard swap; no query observes partial manifest.
  • Incremental rebuild: mutate one file in repo; re-index; verify only one shard is rewritten; unmodified docs retain identical DocMeta.
  • Symbol query path: extract SymbolEntry from Rust fixture; query sym:MyStruct; assert exact (file, line) result.

Differential Testing (Reference Comparison)

  • Corpus: linux kernel headers (~50k files), cpython stdlib (~8k files), synthetic adversarial corpus (unicode, binary-adjacent, long lines).
  • Oracle: grep -rE <pattern> <corpus> (POSIX ERE, line-by-line) — exact match-set ground truth.
  • Protocol: for each pattern in patterns.txt, collect grep line set; collect csearch MatchSpan line set; assert set equality (zero false negatives; log false positive rate).
  • Automation: runs in CI on every PR that touches csearch-search, csearch-regex, csearch-trigquery, csearch-shard.
  • Edge cases covered: zero-length matches, overlapping matches, multibyte UTF-8 codepoints at trigram boundaries, CRLF line endings, files with no newline at EOF.

Property-Based Testing (proptest / arbitrary)

Property Generator Assertion
Trigram-query soundness Random regex (bounded depth) + random ASCII doc candidates ⊇ true_matches (I1)
Posting-list intersection Two random ascending u32 sets gallop_intersect == naive set intersection
Shard encode→decode Random DocId ascending sequence Decoded list == original
Rank score ordering Random SearchResult set top-k heap == sort-then-take
Query parser Random printable strings No panic; error or valid AST

Fuzzing

Fuzz target Input Invariant checked
fuzz_regex_parse Arbitrary bytes as regex string No panic; linear time on matched input
fuzz_shard_reader Arbitrary bytes as shard file No panic; safe error return
fuzz_query_parse Arbitrary bytes as query string No panic
fuzz_trigquery_soundness (regex_bytes, doc_bytes) If regex matches doc, TrigramQuery must evaluate true
fuzz_posting_decode Arbitrary bytes as posting-list segment No panic; decode error or valid list
  • Fuzz infrastructure: cargo-fuzz (libFuzzer) with corpus seeds from differential test corpus.
  • CI: each fuzz target runs 60 seconds per PR; nightly: 8-hour session; crashes committed to fuzz/corpus/.

Chaos and Fault Injection

  • Shard file truncated mid-read → reader returns ShardError::Corrupt; server continues with remaining shards.
  • Manifest points to missing shard → query returns partial results with error annotation; no panic.
  • Concurrent shard hot-reload under query load: verify no torn reads (I7).
  • OOM simulation via allocator shim: index builder must spill to disk rather than abort.
  • Process kill during shard write: shard not visible in manifest until CRC-sealed and atomically linked.

Soak / Endurance

  • 24-hour run: ingest 500 repos, continuous random queries at 100 RPS; assert no memory growth > 10% baseline; no result-set divergence.
  • Incremental churn: modify 1% of files per 10-minute window; verify shard count stays bounded (Phase 12 compaction holds).

Performance Regression

  • Baseline recorded per commit for: index build throughput (MB/s), query P50/P95/P99 latency, candidate reduction ratio.
  • CI fails if P99 query latency increases > 15% vs. baseline on the linux-kernel corpus.
  • Tracked via criterion harmarks; results committed to benches/history/.

Conformance Validation

  • Regex conformance: run PCRE test suite against csearch-regex; assert identical match/no-match outcomes on linear-safe patterns; assert refusal or safe fallback on backtracking-heavy patterns.
  • Shard format version: each Phase 3 format version carries a magic + version byte; reader rejects unknown versions cleanly.

Build & Release Engineering

Toolchain and Reproducibility

Concern Mechanism
Toolchain pin rust-toolchain.toml with exact channel = "stable" + date; all CI nodes use same toolchain
Dependency lock Cargo.lock committed; cargo update only on explicit PRs
Reproducible builds RUSTFLAGS=-C metadata=<hash> + hermetic build environment (Nix flake or Docker image with pinned base)
Cross-compilation cargo build --target x86_64-unknown-linux-musl for static release binaries
Build cache sccache with a shared S3 bucket keyed by toolchain + Cargo.lock hash

CI/CD Topology

  • PR push
    • ├── cargo fmt --check
    • ├── cargo clippy -- -D warnings
    • ├── cargo test --workspace
    • ├── cargo test --workspace --release (integration + differential, corpus subset)
    • ├── fuzz targets (60 s each)
    • └── perf-regression gate (criterion vs. baseline)
  • merge to main
    • ├── all PR steps
    • ├── full differential test (linux + cpython corpus)
    • ├── fuzz targets (8 h, nightly schedule)
    • └── release candidate artifact build
  • tag vX.Y.Z
    • ├── reproducible release build (musl static)
    • ├── artifact signing (sigstore cosign)
    • ├── SBOM generation (cargo-cyclonedx → CycloneDX JSON)
    • └── GitHub Release upload + checksum file

Artifact Signing and Provenance

  • Binaries signed with cosign keyless (OIDC-based); signature and transparency log entry published.
  • cargo-auditable embeds dependency tree into the binary for runtime audit.
  • SBOM: cargo-cyclonedx produces sbom.cdx.json per release; attached to GitHub Release.
  • Build provenance: SLSA level 2 via GitHub Actions provenance attestation.

Dependency Auditing

  • cargo-audit runs on every PR; advisory database checked against RustSec.
  • cargo-deny enforces: license allowlist (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD); duplicate-crate policy (warn); banned crates list (regex crates with backtracking risk).
  • Dependabot weekly PRs for patch-version upgrades; require passing CI + audit.

Version Stamping

  • build.rs in csearch-core embeds CARGO_PKG_VERSION, git SHA (git rev-parse HEAD), build timestamp into const BUILD_INFO.
  • Shard format version baked into ShardHeader.version; reader refuses forward-incompatible versions.
  • CLI csearch stat --version emits BUILD_INFO.

Release Channels

Channel Trigger Audience
nightly main merge Integration testers; carries -nightly suffix
beta tag vX.Y.Z-beta.N Pre-release validation
stable tag vX.Y.Z Production; signed + SBOM

Supply-Chain Verification

  • All dependencies vendored via cargo vendor for air-gapped builds; vendor hash checked against lock.
  • cargo tree --duplicates must be empty for core query path crates.
  • No build.rs in transitive deps may perform network access (enforced via Cargo network offline flag in release builds).

Performance Engineering

Benchmarking Strategy

Benchmark Tool Corpus Target (from constraints)
Index build throughput criterion + wall clock linux-kernel (800 MB) ≥ 50 MB/s on 4-core machine
Query P50 latency criterion + hyperfine 1 M doc index ≤ 50 ms P50
Query P99 latency histogram + hdrhistogram 1 M doc index ≤ 200 ms P99
Posting-list intersection criterion Synthetic: 1 M DocId sets ≥ 500 M DocIds/s gallop
Trigram extraction criterion 100 MB random text ≥ 200 MB/s
Regex verification (PikeVM) criterion 10 k candidates × 1 KB each ≤ 5 µs/candidate
Shard mmap read criterion + perf stat 10 GB shard bandwidth-bound; ≥ 80% of mmap read bandwidth
Shard build (varint+delta encode) criterion 10 M postings ≥ 100 M postings/s

Profiling Methodology

Step Tool Focus
CPU hotspot perf record -g + perf report Find top 3 functions by inclusive samples
Flamegraph cargo-flamegraph → SVG Full call tree; check for unexpected allocator hotpaths
Heap allocation dhat (Valgrind) or heaptrack Per-query allocation count; zero-alloc search path goal
Cache misses perf stat -e cache-misses,cache-references Posting-list random-access pattern; DocMeta table locality
Branch misprediction perf stat -e branch-misses PikeVM tight loop; varint decode branch
SIMD opportunities cargo-show-asm on trigram extraction inner loop Confirm auto-vectorization for byte scan
Lock contention mutrace or tokio-console Server fan-out; shard registry RwLock

Flamegraph Workflow

  1. Build with RUSTFLAGS="-C force-frame-pointers=yes" + --profile=profiling (opt + debuginfo).
  2. Run target workload for 30 s under perf record --call-graph dwarf.
  3. perf script | stackcollapse-perf.pl | flamegraph.pl > flame.svg.
  4. Store flame.svg in benches/flames/<commit-short>/; diff against prior commit using inferno-diff.

Tracing Methodology

  • tracing crate spans on: query parse, TrigramQuery eval, per-shard fan-out, candidate fetch, PikeVM verify, rank, result serialize.
  • Span attributes: n_candidates, n_verified, n_shards, trigram_query_type.
  • Jaeger or OTLP export in server mode; tokio-console for async task analysis.
  • Per-query SearchTrace struct (latency breakdown per stage) emitted at debug log level; used in performance-regression test assertions.

Allocator Analysis

  • Default: std allocator (jemalloc via tikv-jemallocator optional feature for large-corpus benchmarks).
  • Goal: zero heap allocation on the critical search path (posting-list decode, PikeVM verify) — all buffers pre-allocated per query context.
  • Validate with dhat: annotate SearchContext arena; assert dhat::HeapStats.total_bytes_allocated below threshold per query.

Lock Analysis

  • csearch-server shard registry: tokio::sync::RwLock<ShardSet>; reads dominate; write only on manifest reload.
  • Validate contention: tokio-console subscriber; assert rwlock_wait_time < 1 µs P99 per query under 100-connection load.
  • Per-shard fan-out: spawns one task per shard; no shared mutable state during fan-out; results collected via JoinSet.

Cache Analysis

  • Posting lists: access is sequential within a trigram's list (gallop skips forward); prefetch opportunities annotated in PostingReader.
  • DocMeta table: random access by DocId; layout: dense array sorted by DocId for cache-line alignment.
  • Trigram table: binary search over sorted Trigram→offset table; fits in L2 for shards ≤ 500k trigrams.
  • mmap readahead: MADV_SEQUENTIAL hint on posting-list region during build; MADV_RANDOM on doc-meta region during verify.

Throughput and Tail-Latency Targets

Metric Target Condition
Index build ≥ 50 MB/s raw file bytes 4-core, NVMe, 800 MB corpus
Query P50 ≤ 50 ms 1 M docs, 10 shards, typical literal query
Query P95 ≤ 120 ms Same
Query P99 ≤ 200 ms Same
Query P99.9 ≤ 500 ms (deadline enforced) Worst-case .* pattern with result cap
Candidate reduction ≥ 90% false-positive filtering by trigram phase Measured over differential-test corpus
Shard open latency ≤ 5 ms per shard (mmap + header parse) Cold; subsequent queries zero

Memory Profiling

  • Index build: peak RSS bounded by --build-mem-limit flag (default 2 GB); spill to disk when postings buffer exceeds limit.
  • Search path: per-query arena allocated once from SearchContext; freed on drop; no lingering heap after result serialization.
  • mmap resident set: monitored via /proc/self/smaps; assert mmap RSS < 2× active shard size (no double-buffering).
